106 +     recording.container recording.review_timing recording.review_frame_mapping

115 +     feedback.age_reference tracking_results.consumption

117 + )
118 + _LIMIT_FIELDS = (
119 +     "max_document_bytes",
120 +     "max_expanded_epochs",
121 +     "max_prepared_plan_bytes",
122 +     "max_asset_cpu_bytes",
123 +     "max_asset_gpu_bytes",

126 +     "codec_threads_total",

128 +     "decoded_frames_per_instance",
129 +     "decoded_bytes_total",
130 +     "decoder_working_bytes_total",
131 +     "capture_slots",
132 +     "recording_bytes_total",
133 +     "evidence_pending_bytes",

137 + def _paths(root: Path) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
138 +     return (
139 +         root / "config" / "backends" / "visual_stimulus_config.toml",
140 +         root / "contracts" / "policy" / "visual_stimulus_policy.toml",
141 +     )
142 +
143 +
144 + def _load_pair(root: Path) -> LoadedPair:
145 +     config_path, policy_path = _paths(Path(root))
146 +     pair = load_pair(
147 +         config_path,
148 +         policy_path,
149 +         allowed_config_keys=_CONFIG_KEYS,
150 +         allowed_policy_keys=_POLICY_KEYS,
151 +         allowed_empty_tables={
152 +             "projection",
153 +             "programs",
154 +             "assets",
155 +             "idle",
156 +             "video",
157 +             "presentation",
158 +             "photodiode",
159 +             "color",
160 +             "photometric_calibration",
161 +         },
162 +         expected_policy={
163 +             "runtime.process_layout": "coordinator_and_render_worker_with_required_evidence_thread"
164 +         },
165 +     )
166 +     if pair.policy_version != _POLICY_VERSION:
167 +         raise ConfigurationError(
168 +             f"Visual Stimulus policy_version must be {_POLICY_VERSION}"
169 +         )
170 +     if policy_digest(pair.policy) != _POLICY_SHA256:
171 +         raise ConfigurationError(
172 +             "Visual Stimulus fixed policy content differs from implementation"
173 +         )
174 +     return pair
175 +
176 +
177 + def _positive_integer(table: dict[str, Any], name: str, path: str) -> int:
178 +     value = table.get(name)
179 +     if type(value) is not int or value <= 0:
180 +         raise ConfigurationError(f"{path} must be a positive integer")
181 +     return value
182 +
183 +
184 + def _duration_ns(value: object, path: str, scale: int) -> int:
185 +     if type(value) is int:
186 +         decimal = Decimal(value)
187 +     elif isinstance(value, Decimal):
188 +         decimal = value
189 +     else:
190 +         raise ConfigurationError(f"{path} must be positive")
191 +     if decimal <= 0:
192 +         raise ConfigurationError(f"{path} must be positive")
193 +     ns = decimal * scale
194 +     if ns != ns.to_integral_value() or ns >= 1 << 63:
195 +         raise ConfigurationError(
196 +             f"{path} must be an exact positive int64 nanosecond value"
197 +         )
198 +     return int(ns)
199 +
200 +
201 + def _pacing_settings(config: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
202 +     presentation = config.get("presentation", {})
203 +     refresh = presentation.get("pacing_refresh_hz")
204 +     output_id = presentation.get("pacing_output_id")
205 +     if refresh is not None:
206 +         if type(refresh) not in (int, float, Decimal):
207 +             raise ConfigurationError("presentation.pacing_refresh_hz must be positive")
208 +         refresh = float(refresh)
209 +         if not (refresh > 0 and refresh < float("inf")):
210 +             raise ConfigurationError(
211 +                 "presentation.pacing_refresh_hz must be finite and positive"
212 +             )
213 +     if output_id is not None and (
214 +         not isinstance(output_id, str) or not output_id.strip()
215 +     ):
216 +         raise ConfigurationError(
217 +             "presentation.pacing_output_id must be a nonempty output identity"
218 +         )
219 +     return refresh, output_id
220 +
221 +
222 + def load_defaults(root: Path) -> types_pb2.VisualStimulusSettings:
223 +     """Load operator-owned session defaults while preserving optional presence."""
224 +     pair = _load_pair(Path(root))
225 +     config = pair.config
226 +     projection_values(config.get("projection", {}))
227 +     result = types_pb2.VisualStimulusSettings()
228 +     recording = config.get("recording", {})
229 +     if "save_visual_stimulus_data" in recording:
230 +         value = recording["save_visual_stimulus_data"]
231 +         if type(value) is not bool:
232 +             raise ConfigurationError(
233 +                 "recording.save_visual_stimulus_data must be Boolean"
234 +             )
235 +         result.save_visual_stimulus_data = value
236 +     if "ffmpeg_args" in recording:
237 +         args = recording["ffmpeg_args"]
238 +         if not isinstance(args, list) or not all(isinstance(arg, str) for arg in args):
239 +             raise ConfigurationError("recording.ffmpeg_args must be a string array")
240 +         result.review_ffmpeg_args.extend(args)
241 +     return result
242 +
243 +
244 + def load_projection_settings(root: Path | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
245 +     """Read the sole operator source for GUI geometry and diagnostics."""
246 +     pair = _load_pair(root if root is not None else Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3])
247 +     return projection_values(pair.config.get("projection", {}))
248 +
249 +
250 + def load_file_policies(root: Path) -> runtime_pb2.VisualStimulusFilePolicies:
251 +     """Resolve versioned file-only resource and timing policies."""
252 +     pair = _load_pair(Path(root))
253 +     result = runtime_pb2.VisualStimulusFilePolicies(contract_version=CONTRACT_VERSION)
254 +     for key, value in projection_values(pair.config.get("projection", {})).items():
255 +         setattr(result.projection, key, value)
256 +     refresh, output_id = _pacing_settings(pair.config)
257 +     if refresh is not None:
258 +         result.pacing_refresh_hz = refresh
259 +     if output_id is not None:
260 +         result.pacing_output_id = output_id
261 +     limits = pair.config.get("resources", {})
262 +     for field in _LIMIT_FIELDS:
263 +         setattr(
264 +             result.limits, field, _positive_integer(limits, field, f"resources.{field}")
265 +         )
266 +     timing = pair.config.get("recording_runtime", {})
267 +     if "review_scale_factor" in timing:
268 +         result.review_scale_factor = _positive_integer(
269 +             timing, "review_scale_factor", "recording_runtime.review_scale_factor"
270 +         )
271 +     for source, target in (
272 +         ("record_sync_interval_s", "record_sync_interval_ns"),
273 +         ("video_sync_interval_s", "video_sync_interval_ns"),
274 +         ("fragment_target_s", "fragment_target_ns"),
275 +         ("encoder_stall_timeout_s", "encoder_stall_timeout_ns"),
276 +         ("record_stall_timeout_s", "record_stall_timeout_ns"),
277 +     ):
278 +         if source in timing:
279 +             setattr(
280 +                 result,
281 +                 target,
282 +                 _duration_ns(
283 +                     timing[source], f"recording_runtime.{source}", 1_000_000_000
284 +                 ),
285 +             )
286 +     age = pair.config.get("feedback", {}).get("max_result_age_ms")
287 +     if age is not None:
288 +         result.max_result_age_ns = _duration_ns(
289 +             age, "feedback.max_result_age_ms", 1_000_000
290 +         )
291 +     return result
292 +
293 +
294 + def validate_display_profile(
295 +     profile_json: str, *, max_bytes: int = _MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES
296 + ) -> frozenset[str]:
297 +     """Validate the saved display profile without touching graphics devices."""
298 +     profile = parse_display_json(profile_json, max_bytes=max_bytes)
299 +     return frozenset(output.output_id for output in profile.active_outputs)
300 +
301 +
302 + def validate_configuration(
303 +     candidate: types_pb2.ExperimentConfiguration,
304 +     *,
305 +     max_document_bytes: int = _MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES,
306 +     max_expanded_epochs: int = _MAX_EXPANDED_EPOCHS,
307 + ) -> types_pb2.ValidationResult:
308 +     """Validate Visual Stimulus's typed operator settings without opening graphics/media devices."""
309 +     result = types_pb2.ValidationResult(
310 +         completed=True,
311 +         valid=True,
312 +         component="visual_stimulus",
313 +         configuration_module_version="visual-stimulus-config-v2",
314 +     )
315 +     selected = [
316 +         backend
317 +         for backend in candidate.backends
318 +         if backend.backend_name == "visual_stimulus"
319 +     ]
320 +     if len(selected) > 1:
321 +         _issue(
322 +             result,
323 +             "backends.visual_stimulus",
324 +             "DUPLICATE_BACKEND",
325 +             "Visual Stimulus settings appear more than once",
326 +         )
327 +         return result
328 +     if not selected:
329 +         return result
330 +     backend = selected[0]
331 +     if backend.WhichOneof("settings") != "visual_stimulus":
332 +         _issue(
333 +             result,
334 +             "backends.visual_stimulus",
335 +             "MISSING_SETTINGS",
336 +             "Visual Stimulus backend has no typed settings",
337 +         )
338 +         return result
339 +     settings = backend.visual_stimulus
340 +     if settings.HasField("display"):
341 +         try:
342 +             validate_display_profile(
343 +                 settings.display.profile_json, max_bytes=max_document_bytes
344 +             )
345 +         except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
346 +             _issue(
347 +                 result,
348 +                 "backends.visual_stimulus.display.profile_json",
349 +                 "INVALID_DISPLAY_PROFILE",
350 +                 str(exc),
351 +             )
352 +     if (
353 +         settings.HasField("save_visual_stimulus_data")
354 +         and settings.save_visual_stimulus_data
355 +         and not settings.review_ffmpeg_args
356 +     ):
357 +         _issue(
358 +             result,
359 +             "backends.visual_stimulus.review_ffmpeg_args",
360 +             "MISSING_ENCODING_ARGS",
361 +             "saving requires the complete accepted FFmpeg argument list",
362 +         )
363 +
364 +     categories: set[str | None] = set()
365 +     for index, trial in enumerate(candidate.trials):
366 +         prefix = f"trials[{index}].stimulus"
367 +         if not trial.HasField("stimulus") or not trial.stimulus.HasField("program"):
368 +             _issue(
369 +                 result,
370 +                 f"{prefix}.program",
371 +                 "MISSING_PROGRAM",
372 +                 "every trial requires a stimulus program",
373 +             )
374 +             continue
375 +         source = trial.stimulus.program.program_json
376 +         try:
377 +             program = parse_program_json(source, max_bytes=max_document_bytes)
378 +             categories.add(program.category)
379 +             if len(categories) > 1:
380 +                 raise ValueError("All trials must use the same protocol category")
381 +             seed = (
382 +                 trial.stimulus.stimulus_seed_decimal
383 +                 if trial.stimulus.HasField("stimulus_seed_decimal")
384 +                 else "0"
385 +             )
386 +             warnings = validate_program_semantics(
387 +                 program,
388 +                 max_expanded_epochs=max_expanded_epochs,
389 +                 seed_decimal=seed,
390 +             )
391 +             for warning in warnings:
392 +                 issue = result.issues.add(
393 +                     component="visual_stimulus", field_path=f"{prefix}.program"
394 +                 )
395 +                 issue.failure.code = "PREDICTABLE_CLIPPING"
396 +                 issue.failure.message = warning
397 +         except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
398 +             _issue(
399 +                 result, f"{prefix}.program.program_json", "INVALID_PROGRAM", str(exc)
400 +             )
401 +             continue
402 +         if not trial.stimulus.HasField("arena_boundaries"):
403 +             _issue(
404 +                 result,
405 +                 f"{prefix}.arena_boundaries",
406 +                 "MISSING_ARENA_BOUNDARIES",
407 +                 "every trial requires an explicit arena-boundary document",
408 +             )
409 +         else:
410 +             try:
411 +                 parse_json(
412 +                     TrialArenaBoundaries,
413 +                     trial.stimulus.arena_boundaries.boundaries_json,
414 +                     max_bytes=max_document_bytes,
415 +                 )
416 +             except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
417 +                 _issue(
418 +                     result,
419 +                     f"{prefix}.arena_boundaries",
420 +                     "INVALID_ARENA_BOUNDARIES",
421 +                     str(exc),
422 +                 )
423 +         if (
424 +             candidate.HasField("mode")
425 +             and candidate.mode == types_pb2.SESSION_MODE_OPEN_LOOP
426 +             and _program_has_feedback(program)
427 +         ):
428 +             _issue(
429 +                 result,
430 +                 f"{prefix}.program",
431 +                 "FEEDBACK_REQUIRES_CLOSED_LOOP",
432 +                 "open-loop trials cannot declare stimulus feedback",
433 +             )
434 +     return result
435 +
436 +
437 + def _issue(
438 +     result: types_pb2.ValidationResult, field: str, code: str, message: str
439 + ) -> None:
440 +     item = result.issues.add(component="visual_stimulus", field_path=field)
441 +     item.failure.code = code
442 +     item.failure.message = message
443 +     result.valid = False
444 +
445 +
446 + def _program_has_feedback(program: Program) -> bool:
447 +     """Return whether any authored epoch declares a tracking feedback binding."""
448 +
449 +     def nodes_have_feedback(nodes: tuple[Epoch | Group, ...]) -> bool:
450 +         for node in nodes:
451 +             if isinstance(node, Group):
452 +                 if nodes_have_feedback(node.body):
453 +                     return True
454 +             elif any(getattr(setting, "feedback", ()) for setting in node.settings):
455 +                 return True
456 +         return False
457 +
458 +     return nodes_have_feedback(program.sequence)

49   + @pytest.mark.windows
50   + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
51   +     "pattern",
52   +     [
53   +         "grating",
54   +         "polar_grating",
55   +         "checker",
56   +         "dots_grid",
57   +         "dots_rand",
58   +         "noise_binary",
59   +         "noise_gray",
60   +         "filtered_noise",
61   +         "calibration_grid",
62   +         "flat",
63   +     ],
64   + )
65   + def test_native_physical_field_shader_matches_mm_sampler(pattern):
66   +     import numpy as np
67   +
68   +     moderngl = pytest.importorskip("moderngl")
69   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.field_shader import layer_fragment
70   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.shaders import _LAYER_VERTEX
71   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.physical_field import raster_field
72   +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import TextureRecipe
73   +
74   +     params = (
75   +         {"orientation_deg": 37.0, "spatial_period_mm": 10.0, "feature_sharpness": 0.3}
76   +         if pattern == "grating"
77   +         else {}
78   +     )
79   +     if pattern in {"noise_binary", "noise_gray"}:
80   +         params = {"element_size_mm": 3.0, "blur_mm": 0.4, "seed": 192}
81   +     if pattern == "filtered_noise":
82   +         params = {"seed": 534, "black_fraction": 0.3, "edge_smooth_mm": 1.2}
83   +     if pattern == "dots_rand":
84   +         params = {"seed": 2**64 - 59, "blur_mm": 0.4}
85   +     recipe = TextureRecipe(pattern, params, 512, None, None, True)
86   +     context = moderngl.create_standalone_context(require=430)
87   +     owned = []
88   +
89   +     def track(value):
90   +         owned.append(value)
91   +         return value
92   +
93   +     try:
94   +         program = track(
95   +             context.program(
96   +                 vertex_shader=_LAYER_VERTEX, fragment_shader=layer_fragment(recipe)
97   +             )
98   +         )
99   +         buffer = track(context.buffer(struct.pack("8f", 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1)))
100  +         array = track(context.vertex_array(program, [(buffer, "2f", "in_uv")]))
101  +         flags = track(context.buffer(bytes(32)))
102  +         flags.bind_to_storage_buffer(3)
103  +         program["mode"].value = 9
104  +         program["mean_rgb"].value = (0.5,) * 3
105  +         program["modulation_rgb"].value = (0.5,) * 3
106  +         program["opacity"].value = program["contrast"].value = 1.0
107  +         program["clip_vertices"].write(struct.pack("8f", -1, -1, 1, -1, -1, 1, 1, 1))
108  +         for size, extent in (
109  +             ((160, 96), (40, 24)),
110  +             ((200, 80), (40, 24)),
111  +             ((80, 160), (40, 24)),
112  +             ((240, 160), (240, 160)),
113  +         ):
114  +             target = track(context.texture(size, 4, dtype="f4"))
115  +             fbo = track(context.framebuffer([target]))
116  +             fbo.use()
117  +             context.viewport = (0, 0, *size)
118  +             if "field_extent_mm" in program:
119  +                 program["field_extent_mm"].value = extent
120  +             if "field_phase_mm" in program:
121  +                 program["field_phase_mm"].value = (0.137, -0.219)
122  +             from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_draw import draw_layer
123  +
124  +             values = dict(
125  +                 width=extent[0],
126  +                 height=extent[1],
127  +                 period_x=2.0,
128  +                 period_y=3.0,
129  +                 phase_x=0.137 / 2,
130  +                 phase_y=-0.219 / 3,
131  +             )
132  +             setting = NS(
133  +                 kind="texture",
134  +                 grating=False,
135  +                 space=NS(kind="physical_surface"),
136  +                 pattern=NS(kind="image_tile", asset_id="field"),
137  +                 mean_linear_rgb=(0.5,) * 3,
138  +                 modulation_linear_rgb=(0.5,) * 3,
139  +             )
140  +             layouts = tuple(
141  +                 NS(instance_id="field", output_id="out", name=name, binding_id=name)
142  +                 for name in (
143  +                     "clip_vertices_map",
144  +                     "field_extent_mm_map",
145  +                     "field_phase_mm_map",
146  +                 )
147  +             )
148  +             artifact = NS(
149  +                 identity=NS(resource_generation="generation"),
150  +                 display=NS(active_outputs=(NS(output_id="out"),)),
151  +                 uniform_layouts=layouts,
152  +             )
153  +             frame = NS(
154  +                 quad_array=array,
155  +                 layer_program=program,
156  +                 field_programs={"field": (program, array)},
157  +             )
158  +             evidence = draw_layer(
159  +                 moderngl,
160  +                 frame,
161  +                 artifact,
162  +                 {},
163  +                 "out",
164  +                 "map",
165  +                 InstanceSnapshot(
166  +                     "field", "texture", True, tuple(values.items()), setting
167  +                 ),
168  +                 [(-1, -1), (1, -1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)],
169  +             )
170  +             assert {item.binding_id for item in evidence} == {
171  +                 item.binding_id for item in layouts
172  +             }
173  +
174  +             actual = np.frombuffer(target.read(), dtype=np.float32).reshape(
175  +                 size[1], size[0], 4
176  +             )[::-1, :, :3]
177  +             expected = (
178  +                 raster_field(pattern, params, *extent, *size, offset_mm=(0.137, -0.219))
179  +                 / 255
180  +             )
181  +             np.testing.assert_allclose(actual, expected, atol=1.1 / 255, rtol=0)
182  +     finally:
183  +         for item in reversed(owned):
184  +             item.release()
185  +         context.release()

268  +         512,
269  +         None,
270  +         None,
271  +         True,
272  +     )
273  +     image = ImagePixels(
274  +         1,
275  +         1,
276  +         "uint8",
277  +         3,
278  +         ("red", "green", "blue"),
279  +         np.zeros((1, 1, 3), dtype=np.uint8),
280  +         "linear",
281  +         "none",
282  +         recipe,
283  +     )
284  +     from tests.visual_stimulus.support import _geometric_profiles
285  +
286  +     contents = _geometric_profiles(artifact.display)
287  +     artifact = artifact.model_copy(
288  +         update={"geometric_calibration": tuple(contents.values())}
289  +     )
290  +     if not deferred:
291  +         contents["field"] = image
292  +     resources = PreparedResourceBundle(
293  +         PreparedAssetSet((), 0),
294  +         artifact.manifest,
295  +         layouts,
296  +         contents,
297  +         {"field": NS(field_recipe=recipe)} if deferred else {},
298  +     )
299  +     context = moderngl.create_standalone_context(require=430)
300  +     renderer = ModernGLSceneRenderer()
301  +     renderer.bind_graphics_api(moderngl)
302  +     outputs = {
303  +         o.output_id: NS(
304  +             context=context,
305  +             width=o.width_px,
306  +             height=o.height_px,
307  +             bits=8,
308  +             activate=lambda: None,
309  +         )
310  +         for o in artifact.display.active_outputs
311  +     }
312  +     try:
313  +         renderer.prepare_trial(artifact, resources, outputs, {})
314  +         values = dict(
315  +             width=100.0,
316  +             height=100.0,
317  +             x=0.0,
318  +             y=0.0,
319  +             rotation=0.0,
320  +             period_x=1.0,
321  +             period_y=1.0,
322  +             phase_x=0.137,
323  +             phase_y=-0.219,
324  +         )
325  +         snapshot = InstanceSnapshot(
326  +             setting.instance_id, "texture", True, tuple(values.items()), setting
327  +         )
328  +         scene = artifact.source.scenes[0]
329  +         renderer.render_group(
330  +             (scene, False, 0, artifact.identity.trial_id, 0, 0, 123),
331  +             (snapshot,),
332  +             outputs,
333  +             {},
334  +         )
335  +         frame = renderer._outputs[artifact.identity.trial_id]["projector/main"]
336  +         texture = frame.surface_textures[f"map-{face}"]
337  +         width, height = texture.size
338  +         actual = np.frombuffer(texture.read(), dtype="f4").reshape(height, width, 4)[
339  +             ::-1, :, :3
340  +         ]
341  +         expected = (
342  +             raster_field(
343  +                 recipe.pattern,
344  +                 recipe.params,
345  +                 width_mm,
346  +                 height_mm,
347  +                 width,
348  +                 height,
349  +                 offset_mm=(0.137, -0.219),
350  +             )
351  +             / 255
352  +         )
353  +         np.testing.assert_allclose(actual, expected, atol=1.1 / 255, rtol=0)
354  +     finally:
355  +         renderer.release()
356  +         assert (
357  +             not renderer._outputs and not renderer._resource_builder.partial_allocations
358  +         )
359  +         context.release()
360  +

362  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("bits", [8, 10])
363  + def test_projector_window_recreation_preserves_operator_cursor(bits):
364  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import configure_window
365  +
366  +     output = (
367  +         make_prepared_trial()
368  +         .display.active_outputs[0]
369  +         .model_copy(update={"rgb_bits_per_channel": bits})
370  +     )
371  +
372  +     class Windows:
373  +         TRUE, FALSE = 1, 0
374  +         hints = {}
375  +         cursor = (300, 200)
376  +
377  +         def __getattr__(self, name):
378  +             if name.isupper():
379  +                 return name
380  +             raise AttributeError(name)
381  +
382  +         def default_window_hints(self):
383  +             self.hints = {self.CENTER_CURSOR: self.TRUE, self.AUTO_ICONIFY: self.TRUE}
384  +
385  +         def window_hint(self, hint, value):
386  +             self.hints[hint] = value
387  +
388  +         def create_fullscreen(self):
389  +             if self.hints[self.CENTER_CURSOR]:
390  +                 self.cursor = (2560, 360)
391  +
392  +     windows = Windows()
393  +     for _ in range(3):
394  +         configure_window(windows, output)
395  +         windows.create_fullscreen()
396  +         assert windows.cursor == (300, 200)
397  +         assert windows.hints[windows.AUTO_ICONIFY] == windows.FALSE
398  +         assert windows.hints[windows.VISIBLE] == windows.TRUE
399  +         assert windows.hints[windows.RED_BITS] == bits
400  +         assert windows.hints[windows.GREEN_BITS] == bits
401  +         assert windows.hints[windows.BLUE_BITS] == bits
402  +         assert windows.hints[windows.REFRESH_RATE] > 0
403  +

405  + def test_reference_bars_share_exact_pixel_spans_and_restore_scissor():
406  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.calibration_bars import (
407  +         reference_lengths,
408  +         reference_scale,
409  +     )
410  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.reference_bars import draw_reference_bars
411  +
412  +     class Context:
413  +         scissor = (1, 2, 3, 4)
414  +
415  +         def clear(self, *color, alpha, viewport):
416  +             calls.append((self.scissor, color, alpha, viewport))
417  +
418  +     calls = []
419  +     context = Context()
420  +     width, height = 1279, 719
421  +     x, y = reference_lengths(width, height)
422  +     draw_reference_bars(context, width, height)
423  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
424  +     assert calls[-2][0][2] == x
425  +     assert calls[-1][0][3] == y
426  +     assert [rectangle for rectangle, color, _, _ in calls if color == (1, 1, 1)] == [
427  +         (0, 0, width, 2),
428  +         (0, height - 2, width, 2),
429  +         (0, 0, 2, height),
430  +         (width - 2, 0, 2, height),
431  +     ]
432  +     assert all(scissor == viewport for scissor, _, _, viewport in calls)
433  +     scale = reference_scale(width, height, x / 2, y / 2, 1.5)
434  +     assert scale.mm_per_pixel_x == scale.mm_per_pixel_y == 0.5
435  +     assert scale.projector_distance_mm == pytest.approx(width * 0.5 * 1.5)
436  +     calls.clear()
437  +     draw_reference_bars(context, width, height, x_px=200, y_px=100)
438  +     assert calls[-2][0][2] == 200 and calls[-1][0][3] == 100
439  +     assert len(calls) == 8
440  +     direct = reference_scale(width, height, 80, 60, x_px=200, y_px=100)
441  +     assert direct.projector_distance_mm is None
442  +     assert direct.projected_width_mm == pytest.approx(width * 0.4)
443  +     custom = reference_scale(width, height, 80, 60, 1.5, x_px=200, y_px=100)
444  +     assert custom.mm_per_pixel_x == pytest.approx(0.4)
445  +     assert custom.mm_per_pixel_y == pytest.approx(0.6)
446  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
447  +     for length in (0, -1, 1.5, width + 1, float("nan"), float("inf")):
448  +         with pytest.raises(ValueError):
449  +             reference_lengths(width, height, length, 100)
450  +     for invalid in (0, -1, float("nan"), float("inf")):
451  +         with pytest.raises(ValueError):
452  +             reference_scale(width, height, invalid, y / 2, 1.5)
453  +     context.clear = lambda *args, **kwargs: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
454  +         RuntimeError("render failure")
455  +     )
456  +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="render failure"):
457  +         draw_reference_bars(context, width, height)
458  +     assert context.scissor == (1, 2, 3, 4)
459  +

461  + def test_output_blackouts_restore_scissor_on_success_and_failure():
462  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.display_profile import PixelRect
463  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.output_masks import clear_regions
464  +
465  +     context = NS(scissor=(9, 9, 2, 2))
466  +     calls = []
467  +
468  +     def clear(*color, alpha, viewport):
469  +         calls.append((context.scissor, color, alpha, viewport))
470  +
471  +     zone = PixelRect(x=1, y=2, width=3, height=4)
472  +     clear_regions(context, clear, (zone,))
473  +     assert calls == [((1, 2, 3, 4), (0, 0, 0), 1, (1, 2, 3, 4))]
474  +     assert context.scissor == (9, 9, 2, 2)
475  +
476  +     def fail(*args, **kwargs):
477  +         raise RuntimeError("clear failed")
478  +
479  +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="clear failed"):
480  +         clear_regions(context, fail, (zone,))
481  +     assert context.scissor == (9, 9, 2, 2)

484  + def test_idle_uses_prepared_valid_area_union_and_restores_scissor():
485  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.artifact_models import GeometricProfile
486  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.output_masks import clear_valid_background
487  +
488  +     display = make_prepared_trial().display
489  +     output = display.active_outputs[0]
490  +     mappings = [m for m in display.active_mappings if m.output_id == output.output_id]
491  +     profiles = {}
492  +     for mapping in mappings:
493  +         profiles[mapping.mapping_id] = GeometricProfile.model_validate(
494  +             dict(
495  +                 format_version=1,
496  +                 calibration_id="valid-area",
497  +                 mapping_id=mapping.mapping_id,
498  +                 surface_id=mapping.surface_id,
499  +                 output_id=output.output_id,
500  +                 output_width=output.width_px,
501  +                 output_height=output.height_px,
502  +                 viewport=mapping.viewport,
503  +                 rows=2,
504  +                 columns=2,
505  +                 vertices=tuple(
506  +                     dict(uv=p, xy=p) for p in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1))
507  +                 ),
508  +                 orientation="preserving",
509  +                 diagonal="bottom_left_to_top_right",
510  +                 mask=None,
511  +                 weight=None,
512  +                 overlap_group=None,
513  +                 intended_coverage=((0, 0), (1, 0), (1, 1), (0, 1)),
514  +                 coverage_tolerance=1e-6,
515  +                 triangle_area_tolerance=1e-9,
516  +                 valid_area=(0.2, 0.3, 0.8, 0.9),
517  +             )
518  +         )
519  +     calls = []
520  +     context = NS(scissor=(2, 3, 4, 5))
521  +     context.clear = lambda *color, **kwargs: calls.append(
522  +         (context.scissor, color, kwargs)
523  +     )
524  +     clear_valid_background(
525  +         context, (0.3, 0.4, 0.5), output, display, NS(content=profiles)
526  +     )
527  +     assert calls[0][0] is None and calls[0][1] == (0, 0, 0)
528  +     assert len(calls) == len(mappings) + 1
529  +     assert all(c[0] == (20, 30, 60, 60) and c[1] == (0.3, 0.4, 0.5) for c in calls[1:])
530  +     assert context.scissor == (2, 3, 4, 5)
531  +     for key in profiles:
532  +         profiles[key] = profiles[key].model_copy(update={"valid_area": None})
533  +     calls.clear()
534  +     clear_valid_background(
535  +         context, (0.3, 0.4, 0.5), output, display, NS(content=profiles)
536  +     )
537  +     assert len(calls) == 1 and calls[0][1] == (0.3, 0.4, 0.5)

540  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("per_output", [False, True])
541  + def test_photodiode_pulse_renders_after_blackouts_and_black_frame(per_output):
542  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.display_profile import (
543  +         PhotodiodePatch,
544  +         PixelRect,
545  +     )
546  +
547  +     artifact = make_prepared_trial()
548  +     output = artifact.display.active_outputs[0]
549  +     zone = PixelRect(x=0, y=0, width=10, height=10)
550  +     patch = PhotodiodePatch(
551  +         rect=PixelRect(x=12, y=13, width=8, height=6),
552  +         background_rect=PixelRect(x=5, y=6, width=30, height=40),
553  +         high_linear_rgb=(1, 1, 1),
554  +         low_linear_rgb=(0, 0, 0),
555  +     )
556  +     display = artifact.display.model_copy(
557  +         update={
558  +             "outputs": (output.model_copy(update={"blackout_zones": (zone,)}),),
559  +             "photodiode_patch": patch,
560  +             "photodiode_output_id": output.output_id,
561  +         }
562  +     )
563  +     if per_output:
564  +         display = display.model_copy(
565  +             update={
566  +                 "photodiode_output_id": None,
567  +                 "photodiode_patch": None,
568  +                 "photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch},
569  +             }
570  +         )
571  +     artifact = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
572  +     calls = []
573  +     context = NS(scissor=None, screen=None, disable=lambda *_: None)
574  +     program = {
575  +         key: NS(value=None)
576  +         for key in (
577  +             "source_tex",
578  +             "lut_tex",
579  +             "calibrated",
580  +             "lut_size",
581  +             "marker_active",
582  +             "marker_rect",
583  +             "marker_rgb",
584  +         )
585  +     }
586  +     frame = NS(
587  +         context=context,
588  +         output_fbo=NS(clear=lambda *c, **kw: calls.append((c, kw["viewport"]))),
589  +         device_fbo=NS(use=lambda: None),
590  +         output_texture=NS(size=(100, 100), use=lambda *_: None),
591  +         lut_texture=NS(size=(2, 1), use=lambda *_: None),
592  +         output_program=program,
593  +         output_quad_array=NS(render=lambda **_: calls.append("marker/output")),
594  +     )
595  +     renderer = ModernGLSceneRenderer()
596  +     renderer._moderngl = NS(BLEND=1, TRIANGLE_STRIP=5)
597  +     renderer._apply_output(frame, artifact, output.output_id, True, 0)
598  +     assert calls == [
599  +         ((0, 0, 0), (0, 0, 10, 10)),
600  +         ((0, 0, 0), (5, 6, 30, 40)),
601  +         "marker/output",
602  +     ]
603  +     assert program["marker_rect"].value == (0.12, 0.13, 0.2, 0.19)
604  +     assert program["marker_rgb"].value == (1, 1, 1)
605  +     assert context.scissor is None
606  +     if per_output:
607  +         other = output.model_copy(
608  +             update={
609  +                 "output_id": "other",
610  +                 "device_identity": "other",
611  +                 "blackout_zones": (),
612  +             }
613  +         )
614  +         extra = patch.model_copy(
615  +             update={
616  +                 "rect": PixelRect(x=20, y=21, width=2, height=3),
617  +                 "background_rect": None,
618  +             }
619  +         )
620  +         display = display.model_copy(
621  +             update={
622  +                 "outputs": (output, other),
623  +                 "photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch, "other": extra},
624  +             }
625  +         )
626  +         second = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
627  +         calls.clear()
628  +         renderer._apply_output(frame, second, "other", False, 0)
629  +         assert calls == ["marker/output"]
630  +         assert program["marker_rect"].value == (0.2, 0.21, 0.22, 0.24)
631  +         assert program["marker_rgb"].value == (0, 0, 0)
632  +         display = display.model_copy(
633  +             update={"photodiode_patches": {output.output_id: patch}}
634  +         )
635  +         renderer._apply_output(
636  +             frame, artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display}), "other", True, 0
637  +         )
638  +         assert program["marker_active"].value == 0
639  +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="fit inside"):
640  +         PhotodiodePatch.model_validate(
641  +             dict(patch.model_dump(), rect=dict(x=0, y=0, width=1, height=1))
642  +         )
643  +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="exceeds"):
644  +         type(output).model_validate(
645  +             dict(
646  +                 output.model_dump(),
647  +                 blackout_zones=(dict(x=99, y=0, width=2, height=2),),
648  +             )
649  +         )

652  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("matches", [0, 2])
653  + def test_saved_monitor_requires_exact_unique_active_identity(monkeypatch, matches):
654  +     from cephvr.platform.windows import display_identity
655  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort
656  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import DisplayUnavailableError
657  +
658  +     monkeypatch.setattr(display_identity, "monitor_interface", lambda *_: "saved")
659  +     glfw = NS(
660  +         get_monitors=lambda: list(range(matches)),
661  +         get_win32_adapter=lambda monitor: monitor,
662  +         get_win32_monitor=lambda monitor: monitor,
663  +     )
664  +     message = (
665  +         "Windows does not detect the assigned display"
666  +         if matches == 0
667  +         else f"matched {matches} active"
668  +     )
669  +     with pytest.raises(DisplayUnavailableError, match=message):
670  +         ModernGLPort._resolve_monitor("SAVED", glfw)
671  +     glfw.get_monitors = lambda: [17]
672  +     assert ModernGLPort._resolve_monitor("SAVED", glfw) == 17

675  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("mapped", [False, True])
676  + def test_missing_second_monitor_opens_no_windows_and_releases_glfw(tmp_path, mapped):
677  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort
678  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import DisplayUnavailableError
679  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.types import ResourceReleaseReport
680  +
681  +     display = make_prepared_trial().display
682  +     first = display.active_outputs[0]
683  +     second = first.model_copy(
684  +         update={"output_id": "second", "device_identity": "missing"}
685  +     )
686  +     display = display.model_copy(update={"outputs": (first, second)})
687  +     if mapped:
688  +         display = display.model_copy(
689  +             update={
690  +                 "mappings": tuple(
691  +                     mapping.model_copy(update={"output_id": second.output_id})
692  +                     for mapping in display.mappings
693  +                 )
694  +             }
695  +         )
696  +     resolved, terminated = [], []
697  +
698  +     def resolve(identity, glfw):
699  +         resolved.append(identity)
700  +         if identity == "missing":
701  +             raise DisplayUnavailableError("missing saved projector")
702  +         return object()
703  +
704  +     port = ModernGLPort(tmp_path, announce=lambda *_: None, monitor_resolver=resolve)
705  +     # Any window or GL call would fail: identity preflight must precede creation.
706  +     port._glfw = NS(terminate=lambda: terminated.append(True))
707  +     port._moderngl = object()
708  +     with pytest.raises(DisplayUnavailableError, match="missing saved") as failure:
709  +         port.initialize_display(display)
710  +     faces = (
711  +         ", ".join(mapping.surface_id.capitalize() for mapping in display.mappings)
712  +         if mapped
713  +         else "second"
714  +     )
715  +     assert f"{faces} projector: missing saved projector" in str(failure.value)
716  +     assert "Saved display: missing (output second)" in str(failure.value)
717  +     assert "Refresh in Devices > Projectors" in failure.value.remedy
718  +     assert resolved == [first.device_identity, "missing"]
719  +     assert terminated == [True]
720  +     assert not port._outputs and not port._window_obligations
721  +     assert port.release() == ResourceReleaseReport(("visual_stimulus:glfw",))
722  +
723  +
724  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("cleanup_fails", [False, True])
725  + def test_wrong_adapter_rejection_requires_native_context_and_window_release(
726  +     monkeypatch, tmp_path, cleanup_fails
727  + ):
728  +     from cephvr.platform.windows import nvidia_device
729  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering import native
730  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import (
731  +         DisplayGpuUnavailableError,
732  +         NativeRenderingError,
733  +     )
734  +
735  +     selected = nvidia_device.NvidiaDevice(0, "GPU-board", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti")
736  +     monkeypatch.setattr(nvidia_device, "sys", NS(platform="win32"))
737  +     monkeypatch.setattr(nvidia_device, "_enumerate_cuda_devices", lambda: [selected])
738  +     released, destroyed, terminated = [], [], []
739  +
740  +     def release():
741  +         released.append(True)
742  +         if cleanup_fails and len(released) == 1:
743  +             raise RuntimeError("context release unconfirmed")
744  +
745  +     context = NS(
746  +         info={"GL_VENDOR": "ATI Technologies Inc.", "GL_RENDERER": "AMD Radeon"},
747  +         screen=object(),
748  +         release=release,
749  +     )
750  +     monkeypatch.setattr(native, "attach_window_context", lambda _: context)
751  +     monkeypatch.setattr(native, "configure_window", lambda *_: None)
752  +     port = native.ModernGLPort(
753  +         tmp_path, announce=lambda *_: None, monitor_resolver=lambda *_: object()
754  +     )
755  +     port._glfw = NS(
756  +         create_window=lambda *_: object(),
757  +         get_current_context=lambda: None,
758  +         make_context_current=lambda *_: None,
759  +         destroy_window=destroyed.append,
760  +         terminate=lambda: terminated.append(True),
761  +     )
762  +     port._moderngl = object()
763  +     expected = NativeRenderingError if cleanup_fails else DisplayGpuUnavailableError
764  +     with pytest.raises(expected, match="AMD Radeon") as failure:
765  +         port.initialize_display(make_prepared_trial().display)
766  +     assert isinstance(failure.value, DisplayGpuUnavailableError) is not cleanup_fails
767  +     assert bool(port._outputs) is cleanup_fails
768  +     if cleanup_fails:
769  +         assert not destroyed and not terminated
770  +         assert not port.release().outstanding
771  +     assert destroyed and terminated and not port._outputs
772  +
773  +
774  + def test_linear_rate_integral_and_keyframe_evaluation() -> None:
775  +     ramp = NS(kind="ramp", initial=0.0, slope_per_s=0.5)
776  +     assert integrate_function(ramp, 0, 2_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(1.0)
777  +     keyframes = NS(
778  +         kind="keyframes",
779  +         interpolation="linear",
780  +         knots=(
781  +             NS(time=NS(seconds="0", ns=lambda: 0), value=0.0),
782  +             NS(time=NS(seconds="2", ns=lambda: 2_000_000_000), value=4.0),
783  +         ),
784  +     )
785  +     assert evaluate_function(keyframes, 1_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(2.0)
786  +     assert integrate_function(keyframes, 0, 2_000_000_000) == pytest.approx(4.0)

833  + def test_projected_layer_vertices_are_retained_as_exact_shader_words() -> None:
834  +     corners = ((-1.0, -0.5), (0.5, -0.5), (0.5, 1.0), (-1.0, 1.0))

842  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("mode", ["landmarks", "every_frame"])
843  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("pulse_enabled", [True, False])
844  + @pytest.mark.parametrize("multiple", [False, True])
845  + def test_engine_freezes_exact_provider_evidence_for_recording(
846  +     pulse_enabled, multiple, mode
847  + ) -> None:
848  +     from dataclasses import replace
849  +
850  +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.types import ImageSnapshot
851  +
852  +     artifact = make_prepared_trial()
853  +     if not pulse_enabled:
854  +         artifact = artifact.model_copy(
855  +             update={
856  +                 "display": artifact.display.model_copy(
857  +                     update={
858  +                         "photodiode_enabled": False,
859  +                         "pacing_output_id": artifact.display.photodiode_output_id,
860  +                         "photodiode_output_id": "projector/disconnected",
861  +                     }
862  +                 )
863  +             }
864  +         )
865  +     artifact = artifact.model_copy(
866  +         update={
867  +             "display": artifact.display.model_copy(update={"photodiode_mode": mode})
868  +         }
869  +     )
870  +     display = artifact.display
871  +     output = display.outputs[0]
872  +     if multiple:
873  +         outputs = (
874  +             output,
875  +             output.model_copy(
876  +                 update={"output_id": "second", "device_identity": "second"}
877  +             ),
878  +             output.model_copy(
879  +                 update={"output_id": "blank", "device_identity": "blank"}
880  +             ),
881  +         )
882  +         display = display.model_copy(
883  +             update={
884  +                 "outputs": outputs,
885  +                 "photodiode_output_id": None,
886  +                 "photodiode_patch": None,
887  +                 "photodiode_patches": {
888  +                     o.output_id: artifact.display.photodiode_patch for o in outputs[:2]
889  +                 },
890  +                 "pacing_output_id": output.output_id,
891  +             }
892  +         )
893  +         artifact = artifact.model_copy(update={"display": display})
894  +     outputs = display.active_outputs
895  +
896  +     class Port:
897  +         def initialize_display(self, selected):
898  +             return DisplayInitialization(
899  +                 tuple(o.output_id for o in outputs),
900  +                 tuple((o.output_id, 8, 8, 8) for o in outputs),
901  +                 tuple((o.output_id, o.width_px, o.height_px) for o in outputs),
902  +                 tuple(
903  +                     (o.output_id, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id))
904  +                     for o in outputs
905  +                 ),
906  +                 tuple(
907  +                     OutputActivity(
908  +                         o.output_id, 1, 2, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
909  +                     )
910  +                     for o in outputs
911  +                 ),
912  +             )
913  +
914  +         def prepare_trial(self, prepared, resources):
915  +             assert prepared == artifact
916  +             assert resources is not None
917  +
918  +         def select_images(self, trial_id, epoch_index, snapshots):
919  +             image = ImageSnapshot(
920  +                 snapshots[0].instance_id,
921  +                 "next",
922  +                 1,
923  +                 "previous",
924  +                 0,
925  +                 "residency_hold",
926  +                 True,
927  +             )
928  +             return (replace(snapshots[0], image_selection=image), *snapshots[1:])
929  +
930  +         def render(self, scene, state):
931  +             return RenderPassResult(
932  +                 tuple(
933  +                     RenderedOutput(o.output_id, o.width_px, o.height_px, 8, object())
934  +                     for o in outputs
935  +                 ),
936  +                 (),
937  +                 (),
938  +                 (),
939  +             )
940  +
941  +         def present(self, frames):
942  +             return tuple(
943  +                 OutputActivity(
944  +                     o.output_id, 10, 11, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
945  +                 )
946  +                 for o in outputs
947  +             )
948  +
949  +         def show_idle(self, selected):
950  +             return tuple(
951  +                 OutputActivity(
952  +                     o.output_id, 20, 21, display.requested_swap_interval(o.output_id)
953  +                 )
954  +                 for o in outputs
955  +             )
956  +
957  +     engine = RendererEngine(
958  +         Port(), clock_ns=lambda: 0, resource_provider=lambda _: object()
959  +     )
960  +     engine.initialize_display(display)
961  +     engine.prepare_trial(artifact)
962  +     cancelled_idle = engine.stop_trial()
963  +     assert {o.output_id for o in cancelled_idle} == {o.output_id for o in outputs}
964  +     engine.begin_trial(artifact.identity.trial_id, 0)
965  +     update = engine.render_tick(0)
966  +
967  +     assert update.evidence_state.scene_id == update.group.scene_id
968  +     assert update.evidence_state.evaluation_host_ns == 0
969  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].image_index == 0
970  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].requested_image_index == 1
971  +     assert update.evidence_state.images[0].repeated
972  +     assert update.evidence_state.active_instance_ids
973  +     assert len(update.evidence_submissions) == len(outputs)
974  +     for submission in update.evidence_submissions:
975  +         assert submission.phase == "returned"
976  +         expected_marker = submission.output_id in display.marker_patches
977  +         assert submission.marker_high == (
978  +             update.group.photodiode_high if expected_marker else None
979  +         )
980  +         assert submission.marker_index == (0 if expected_marker else None)
981  +     if not pulse_enabled:
982  +         assert update.group.photodiode_high is None
983  +     next_update = engine.render_tick(1)
984  +     if pulse_enabled:
985  +         assert next_update.group.photodiode_high == (mode == "landmarks")
986  +     stopped_idle = engine.stop_trial(2)
987  +     assert {o.output_id for o in stopped_idle} == {o.output_id for o in outputs}
988  +

1007 + def test_arena_model_and_physical_projection_are_finite_and_pose_sensitive():
1008 +     settings = NS(
1009 +         fixed_height_mm=100.0,
1010 +         fixed_pitch_deg=0.0,
1011 +         fixed_roll_deg=0.0,
1012 +         asset_to_world=(
1013 +             (1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1014 +             (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1015 +             (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
1016 +             (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
1017 +         ),
1018 +     )
1019 +     origin = arena_model_matrix(settings, {"x": 0.0, "y": 0.0, "yaw": 0.0})
1020 +     moved = arena_model_matrix(settings, {"x": 25.0, "y": -5.0, "yaw": 30.0})
1021 +     assert origin[0][3] == 0.0 and origin[1][3] == 0.0
1022 +     assert moved[0][3] == 25.0 and moved[1][3] == -5.0
1023 +     assert moved[0][0] == pytest.approx(3**0.5 / 2)
1024 +     assert len(gl_matrix_words(moved)) == 16

1026 +     display = make_prepared_trial().display

1029 +     assert len(projection) == 4 and all(len(row) == 4 for row in projection)
1030 +     assert all(value == pytest.approx(value) for row in projection for value in row)

1032 +
1033 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1034 +     "angles", [(0, 0, 0), (90, 0, 0), (35, -20, 17), (-175, 80, -110)]
1035 + )
1036 + def test_effective_arena_pose_matches_rendered_rigid_transform(angles):
1037 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.arena import effective_arena_pose
1038 +
1039 +     yaw, pitch, roll = angles
1040 +     settings = NS(
1041 +         kind="arena",
1042 +         instance_id="arena",
1043 +         world_frame_id="world",
1044 +         fixed_height_mm=12.0,
1045 +         fixed_pitch_deg=pitch,
1046 +         fixed_roll_deg=roll,
1047 +         asset_to_world=((1, 0, 0, 8), (0, 1, 0, 9), (0, 0, 1, 10), (0, 0, 0, 1)),
1048 +     )
1049 +     state = {"x": 2.0, "y": -3.0, "yaw": yaw}
1050 +     snapshot = InstanceSnapshot("arena", "arena", True, tuple(state.items()), settings)
1051 +     pose = effective_arena_pose(snapshot)
1052 +     assert pose.instance_id == "arena" and pose.frame_id == "world"
1053 +     assert pose.position_mm == (2.0, -3.0, 12.0)
1054 +     x, y, z, w = pose.orientation_xyzw
1055 +     assert x * x + y * y + z * z + w * w == pytest.approx(1)
1056 +     rotation = (
1057 +         (1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - z * w), 2 * (x * z + y * w)),
1058 +         (2 * (x * y + z * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - x * w)),
1059 +         (2 * (x * z - y * w), 2 * (y * z + x * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y)),
1060 +     )
1061 +     model = arena_model_matrix(settings, state)
1062 +     for row in range(3):
1063 +         assert model[row][:3] == pytest.approx(rotation[row])
1064 +         assert model[row][3] == pytest.approx(
1065 +             pose.position_mm[row]
1066 +             + sum(rotation[row][j] * settings.asset_to_world[j][3] for j in range(3))
1067 +         )

1103 + def test_clipping_diagnostic_reservation_and_flag_decode_are_bounded():
1104 +     assert DIAGNOSTIC_SLOTS == 16
1105 +     assert reservation_bytes(4) == 4 * 16 * 16
1106 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError):
1107 +         reservation_bytes(-1)

1112 +
1113 + def test_gpu_diagnostic_ring_preserves_group_order_across_slot_reuse(monkeypatch):
1114 +     import sys
1115 +     from types import SimpleNamespace
1116 +
1117 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.diagnostics import DiagnosticRing
1118 +
1119 +     class Buffer:
1120 +         def __init__(self):
1121 +             self.glo = id(self)
1122 +
1123 +         def write(self, data):
1124 +             assert len(data) == 16
1125 +
1126 +         def read(self, size):
1127 +             assert size == 16
1128 +             return bytes(16)
1129 +
1130 +     class Context:
1131 +         def buffer(self, *, reserve):
1132 +             assert reserve == 16
1133 +             return Buffer()
1134 +
1135 +     statuses = {}
1136 +     sequence = iter(range(1, 100))
1137 +     gl = SimpleNamespace(
1138 +         GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BARRIER_BIT=1,
1139 +         GL_BUFFER_UPDATE_BARRIER_BIT=2,
1140 +         GL_SHADER_STORAGE_BUFFER=3,
1141 +         GL_SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE=4,
1142 +         GL_ALREADY_SIGNALED=5,
1143 +         GL_CONDITION_SATISFIED=6,
1144 +         GL_WAIT_FAILED=7,
1145 +         glBindBufferBase=lambda *_: None,
1146 +         glMemoryBarrier=lambda *_: None,
1147 +         glFenceSync=lambda *_: next(sequence),
1148 +         glFlush=lambda: None,
1149 +         glClientWaitSync=lambda sync, *_: statuses[sync],
1150 +         glDeleteSync=lambda *_: None,
1151 +     )
1152 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", SimpleNamespace(GL=gl))
1153 +     ring = DiagnosticRing(Context(), lambda item: item)
1154 +     for group_id in (0, 1):
1155 +         ring.begin(group_id, "output", 0, group_id)
1156 +         ring.end()
1157 +     statuses.update({1: 5, 2: 0})
1158 +     assert [item.group_id for item in ring.poll()] == [0]
1159 +     ring.begin(2, "output", 0, 2)
1160 +     ring.end()
1161 +     statuses.update({3: 5})
1162 +     assert ring.poll() == ()
1163 +     statuses[2] = 5
1164 +     assert [item.group_id for item in ring.poll()] == [1, 2]
1165 +     ring.begin(3, "output", 0, 3)
1166 +     ring.end()
1167 +     statuses[4] = 7
1168 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="fence wait failed"):
1169 +         ring.poll()
1170 +     ring.release()
1171 +
1172 +

1201 +         layer_setting.instance_id,
1202 +         "texture",
1203 +         True,
1204 +         (
1205 +             ("x", 0.0),
1206 +             ("y", 0.0),
1207 +             ("rotation", 0.0),

1209 +             ("height", 20.0),

1219 +         kind="arena",

1224 +         asset_to_world=(
1225 +             (1.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1226 +             (0.0, 1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
1227 +             (0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
1228 +             (0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
1229 +         ),

1233 +     )
1234 +     scene = NS(arena_instance_id="arena", layer_instance_ids=(layer.instance_id,))
1235 +     events = []

1238 +
1239 +     class Uniform:
1240 +         def __init__(self):
1241 +             self.value = None
1242 +             self.bytes = None
1243 +
1244 +         def write(self, value):
1245 +             self.bytes = value

1249 +             return self.setdefault(key, Uniform())

1253 +             assert mode == renderer._moderngl.TRIANGLE_STRIP
1254 +             events.append("layer")

1259 +         events.append("arena")

1264 +         frame,

1266 +         mapping.output_id,
1267 +         mapping,
1268 +         scene,
1269 +         {"arena": arena, layer.instance_id: layer},
1270 +         {},
1271 +         uniforms,
1272 +     )
1273 +     assert events == ["arena", "layer"]
1274 +     assert arena_layout.binding_id in uniforms
1275 +     assert len(uniforms[arena_layout.binding_id].words) == 16
1276 +     assert clip_layout.binding_id in uniforms
1277 +     assert frame.layer_program["clip_vertices"].bytes is not None

1296 +         mapping.output_id,
1297 +         mapping,
1298 +         NS(arena_instance_id=None, layer_instance_ids=(setting.instance_id,)),
1299 +         {setting.instance_id: snapshot},
1300 +         {},
1301 +         uniforms,
1302 +     )
1303 +     assert not uniforms
1304 +
1305 +
1306 + def _layout(instance_id, name, output_id="out", binding_id=None):
1307 +     return NS(
1308 +         instance_id=instance_id,
1309 +         output_id=output_id,
1310 +         name=name,
1311 +         binding_id=binding_id or f"{instance_id}:{name}",
1312 +     )

1315 + def test_draw_layer_gl_values_match_evidence_words_and_lookups_fail_clearly():
1316 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.evidence import uniform_values
1317 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_draw import draw_layer
1318 +
1319 +     class Uniform:
1320 +         value = None
1321 +
1322 +         def write(self, value):
1323 +             pass
1324 +
1325 +     class Program(dict):
1326 +         def __getitem__(self, key):
1327 +             return self.setdefault(key, Uniform())
1328 +
1329 +     names = ("opacity", "contrast", "grating_sharpness", "mean_rgb", "modulation_rgb")
1330 +     names += (
1331 +         "frequency",
1332 +         "phase",
1333 +         "period",
1334 +         "clip_vertices_map",
1335 +     )
1336 +     layouts = tuple(_layout("layer", name) for name in names)
1337 +     setting = NS(
1338 +         kind="texture",
1339 +         grating=True,
1340 +         space=NS(kind="angular"),
1341 +         pattern=NS(kind="sine_grating", feature_sharpness=0.25),
1342 +         mean_linear_rgb=(0.1, 0.2, 0.3),
1343 +         modulation_linear_rgb=(0.4, 0.5, 0.6),
1344 +     )
1345 +     state = (
1346 +         ("width", 20.0),
1347 +         ("height", 30.0),
1348 +         ("frequency", 0.125),
1349 +         ("phase_x", 0.5),
1350 +         ("phase_y", 0.25),
1351 +         ("opacity", 0.75),
1352 +         ("contrast", 0.5),
1353 +     )
1354 +     snapshot = InstanceSnapshot("layer", "texture", True, state, setting)
1355 +     artifact = NS(
1356 +         identity=NS(resource_generation="g"),
1357 +         display=NS(active_outputs=(NS(output_id="out"),)),
1358 +         uniform_layouts=layouts,
1359 +     )
1360 +     program = Program()
1361 +     frame = NS(
1362 +         quad_array=NS(render=lambda mode: None),
1363 +         layer_program=program,
1364 +         field_programs={},
1365 +     )
1366 +
1367 +     def draw(layouts_):
1368 +         return draw_layer(
1369 +             NS(TRIANGLE_STRIP=5),
1370 +             frame,
1371 +             NS(**{**vars(artifact), "uniform_layouts": layouts_}),
1372 +             {},
1373 +             "out",
1374 +             "map",
1375 +             snapshot,
1376 +             [(-1, -1), (1, -1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)],
1377 +         )
1378 +
1379 +     drawn = draw(layouts)
1380 +     assert [item.binding_id for item in drawn] == ["layer:clip_vertices_map"]
1381 +     recorded = uniform_values(artifact, snapshot)
1382 +     for name in names[:-1]:
1383 +         written = program[name].value
1384 +         written = (written,) if isinstance(written, float) else written
1385 +         assert recorded[f"layer:{name}"].words == float32_words(written)
1386 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="omits the mapped surface clip uniform"):
1387 +         draw(layouts[:-1])

1390 + def test_layer_cache_reuses_static_frames_and_never_serves_another_artifact(
1391 +     monkeypatch,
1392 + ):
1393 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering import layer_cache
1394 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_cache import LayerCache
1395 +
1396 +     calls = []
1397 +     monkeypatch.setattr(
1398 +         layer_cache,
1399 +         "screen_image_frame",
1400 +         lambda profile: (
1401 +             calls.append(("frame", profile.scale)) or ([(0, 0)] * 4, (8, 4))
1402 +         ),
1403 +     )
1404 +     monkeypatch.setattr(
1405 +         layer_cache,
1406 +         "calibrated_fold_map",
1407 +         lambda geometry, profiles, profile: (
1408 +             calls.append(("fold", profile.scale)) or (profile.scale,) * 6
1409 +         ),
1410 +     )
1411 +
1412 +     def artifact(scale):
1413 +         return NS(
1414 +             uniform_layouts=(_layout("i", "clip_vertices_m"),),
1415 +             geometric_calibration=(NS(mapping_id="m", scale=scale),),
1416 +             display=NS(geometry=None),
1417 +         )
1418 +
1419 +     first, second = artifact(1.0), artifact(2.0)
1420 +     cache = LayerCache(first)
1421 +     assert cache.screen_frame("m") == cache.screen_frame("m")
1422 +     assert cache.fold_map("m") == cache.fold_map("m") == (1.0,) * 6
1423 +     assert calls == [("frame", 1.0), ("fold", 1.0)]
1424 +     # A new Setup yields a new artifact: the owner's old cache is ignored.
1425 +     fresh = LayerCache.for_artifact(second, cache)
1426 +     assert fresh is not cache and LayerCache.for_artifact(first, cache) is cache
1427 +     assert fresh.fold_map("m") == (2.0,) * 6
1428 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="geometric calibration for mapping x"):
1429 +         cache.fold_map("x")
1430 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="omits field_extent_mm_m"):
1431 +         cache.layout("i", "out", "field_extent_mm_m")
1432 +

1434 + def test_field_programs_compile_once_per_distinct_recipe():
1435 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.scene_resources import compile_field_programs
1436 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.texture_recipe import TextureRecipe
1437 +
1438 +     def recipe(seed):
1439 +         return TextureRecipe("noise_binary", {"seed": seed}, 512, None, None, True)
1440 +
1441 +     compiled, tracked = [], []
1442 +
1443 +     class Context:
1444 +         def program(self, *, vertex_shader, fragment_shader):
1445 +             compiled.append(fragment_shader)
1446 +             return NS(fragment=fragment_shader)
1447 +
1448 +         def vertex_array(self, program, content):
1449 +             return NS(program=program, content=content)
1450 +
1451 +     def track(resource):
1452 +         tracked.append(resource)
1453 +         return resource
1454 +
1455 +     programs = compile_field_programs(
1456 +         Context(),
1457 +         {"a": recipe(1), "b": recipe(1), "c": recipe(2)},
1458 +         "quad",
1459 +         track,
1460 +     )
1461 +     assert len(compiled) == len(set(compiled)) == 2
1462 +     assert programs["a"] is programs["b"] and programs["a"] != programs["c"]
1463 +     assert programs["c"][0].fragment != programs["a"][0].fragment
1464 +     assert len(tracked) == 4  # each program/array is owned (and released) once
1465 +

1467 + def test_native_submission_orders_marker_last_and_preserves_failed_attempt():
1468 +     from pathlib import Path
1469 +
1470 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort
1471 +
1472 +     artifact = make_prepared_trial()
1473 +     marker = artifact.display.outputs[0]
1474 +     other = marker.model_copy(
1475 +         update={"output_id": "projector/second", "device_identity": "second"}
1476 +     )
1477 +     display = artifact.display.model_copy(update={"outputs": (marker, other)})
1478 +     events = []
1479 +
1480 +     class GLFW:
1481 +         def swap_buffers(self, window):
1482 +             events.append(window)
1483 +             if window == "second":
1484 +                 raise RuntimeError("device lost")
1485 +
1486 +     port = ModernGLPort(Path("."), announce=lambda *_: None, clock_ns=lambda: 5)
1487 +     port._glfw = GLFW()
1488 +     port._display = display
1489 +     port._outputs = {
1490 +         marker.output_id: NS(
1491 +             window="marker",
1492 +             swap_interval=1,
1493 +             activate=lambda: None,
1494 +             context=NS(finish=lambda: events.append("finish:marker")),
1495 +         ),
1496 +         other.output_id: NS(
1497 +             window="second",
1498 +             swap_interval=0,
1499 +             activate=lambda: None,
1500 +             context=NS(finish=lambda: events.append("finish:second")),
1501 +         ),
1502 +     }
1503 +     observed = port.present(
1504 +         (NS(output_id=marker.output_id), NS(output_id=other.output_id))
1505 +     )
1506 +     # Only the VSync output drains its flip before the return timestamp.
1507 +     assert events == ["second", "marker", "finish:marker"]
1508 +     assert observed[0].error == "VISUAL_STIMULUS_SWAP:device lost"
1509 +     assert observed[1].error is None
1510 +     assert observed[0].swap_entry_ns == observed[0].swap_return_ns == 5
1511 +

1513 + def test_native_window_attachment_preserves_glfw_context_ownership():
1514 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native_display import (
1515 +         NativeRenderingError,
1516 +         attach_window_context,
1517 +     )
1518 +
1519 +     contexts = []
1520 +
1521 +     class GraphicsAPI:
1522 +         def init_context(self):
1523 +             contexts.append(NS(version_code=430, release=lambda: None))
1524 +
1525 +         def get_context(self):
1526 +             return contexts[-1]
1527 +
1528 +     api = GraphicsAPI()
1529 +     first = attach_window_context(api)
1530 +     second = attach_window_context(api)
1531 +     assert first is not second
1532 +     releases = []
1533 +     invalid = NS(version_code=420, release=lambda: releases.append("wrapper"))
1534 +     with pytest.raises(NativeRenderingError, match="OpenGL 4.3"):

1536 +             NS(init_context=lambda: None, get_context=lambda: invalid)

1538 +     assert releases == ["wrapper"]

1541 + def test_native_failed_gpu_release_keeps_context_for_cleanup_retry():
1542 +     from pathlib import Path
1543 +
1544 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.native import ModernGLPort, _OutputContext
1545 +
1546 +     calls = []
1547 +
1548 +     class Resource:
1549 +         failing = True
1550 +
1551 +         def release(self):
1552 +             if self.failing:
1553 +                 raise RuntimeError("release pending")
1554 +             calls.append("gpu")
1555 +
1556 +     port = ModernGLPort(Path("."), announce=lambda *_: None)
1557 +     port._glfw = NS(
1558 +         make_context_current=lambda _: None,
1559 +         get_current_context=lambda: None,
1560 +         destroy_window=lambda _: calls.append("window"),
1561 +         terminate=lambda: calls.append("glfw"),
1562 +     )
1563 +     resource = Resource()
1564 +     port._resources["prepared:image"] = resource
1565 +     port._outputs["screen"] = _OutputContext(
1566 +         "screen",
1567 +         object(),
1568 +         NS(release=lambda: calls.append("context")),
1569 +         1,
1570 +         1,
1571 +         8,
1572 +         1,
1573 +         None,
1574 +         lambda: None,
1575 +     )
1576 +     port._window_obligations.add("screen")
1577 +     first = port.release()
1578 +     assert first.outstanding and calls == []
1579 +     resource.failing = False
1580 +     second = port.release()
1581 +     assert not second.outstanding
1582 +     assert calls == ["gpu", "context", "window", "glfw"]
1583 +     assert {
1584 +         "visual_stimulus:gpu:prepared:image",
1585 +         "visual_stimulus:window:screen",
1586 +         "visual_stimulus:glfw",
1587 +     } <= set(second.released)
1588 +

1710 + @pytest.mark.parametrize("source,target", [(2.0, 1.0), (1.0, 2.0), (1.0, 1.0)])
1711 + def test_image_fit_uv_spans_preserve_aspect_and_center(source, target):
1712 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.image_fit import fitted_uv_scale
1713 +
1714 +     contain = fitted_uv_scale("contain", source, target)
1715 +     cover = fitted_uv_scale("cover", source, target)
1716 +     assert min(contain) == 1.0 and max(contain) >= 1.0
1717 +     assert max(cover) == 1.0 and min(cover) <= 1.0
1718 +     assert target * contain[1] / contain[0] == pytest.approx(source)
1719 +     assert target * cover[1] / cover[0] == pytest.approx(source)
1720 +     assert fitted_uv_scale("stretch", source, target) == (1.0, 1.0)

1777 + @pytest.mark.windows
1778 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1779 +     "fit,face",
1780 +     [("resize", "front"), ("source", "front"), ("fold", "front"), ("fold", "bottom")],
1781 + )
1782 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1783 +     "size,target", [((7, 9), (4, 6)), ((3, 3), (6, 8)), ((7, 3), (4, 6))]
1784 + )
1785 + def test_native_source_shader_preserves_pixels_with_center_crop_and_padding(
1786 +     size, target, fit, face
1787 + ):
1788 +     import moderngl
1789 +     import numpy as np
1790 +
1791 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.image_fit import source_rectangles
1792 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.layer_draw import draw_layer
1793 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.shaders import _LAYER_FRAGMENT, _LAYER_VERTEX
1794 +
1795 +     context = moderngl.create_standalone_context(require=430)
1796 +     try:
1797 +         program = context.program(
1798 +             vertex_shader=_LAYER_VERTEX, fragment_shader=_LAYER_FRAGMENT
1799 +         )
1800 +         vertices = context.buffer(
1801 +             np.array([0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1], dtype="f4").tobytes()
1802 +         )
1803 +         quad = context.vertex_array(program, [(vertices, "2f", "in_uv")])
1804 +         pixels = np.arange(size[0] * size[1] * 4, dtype="f4").reshape(
1805 +             size[1], size[0], 4
1806 +         )
1807 +         pixels /= pixels.size
1808 +         pixels[:, :, 3] = 1
1809 +         texture = context.texture(size, 4, pixels[::-1].tobytes(), dtype="f4")
1810 +         texture.filter = (moderngl.NEAREST, moderngl.NEAREST)
1811 +         texture.use(0)
1812 +         flags = context.buffer(reserve=64)
1813 +         flags.bind_to_storage_buffer(3)
1814 +         target_fbo = context.simple_framebuffer(target, components=4, dtype="f4")
1815 +         target_fbo.use()
1816 +         layouts = tuple(
1817 +             NS(
1818 +                 instance_id="image",
1819 +                 output_id="out",
1820 +                 binding_id=name,
1821 +                 name=f"{name}_map",
1822 +             )
1823 +             for name in (
1824 +                 ("clip_vertices", "image_target_pixels", "image_fold_map")
1825 +                 if fit == "fold"
1826 +                 else ("clip_vertices", "image_target_pixels")
1827 +             )
1828 +         )
1829 +         profile = NS(
1830 +             mapping_id="map",
1831 +             surface_id="front",
1832 +             viewport=NS(x=0, y=0, width=target[0], height=target[1]),
1833 +             valid_area=(0, 0, 1, 1),
1834 +             output_width=target[0],
1835 +             output_height=target[1],
1836 +             columns=2,
1837 +             vertices=tuple(NS(uv=uv, xy=uv) for uv in ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1))),
1838 +         )
1839 +         front = NS(**vars(profile))
1840 +         front.mapping_id = "reference"
1841 +         if face == "bottom":
1842 +             profile.surface_id = "bottom"
1843 +             profile.vertices = tuple(
1844 +                 NS(uv=uv, xy=xy)
1845 +                 for uv, xy in zip(
1846 +                     ((0, 1), (0, 0), (1, 1), (1, 0)),
1847 +                     ((0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1)),
1848 +                     strict=True,
1849 +                 )
1850 +             )
1851 +         artifact = NS(
1852 +             identity=NS(resource_generation="generation"),
1853 +             display=NS(
1854 +                 active_outputs=(NS(output_id="out"),),
1855 +                 geometry=NS(
1856 +                     surfaces=tuple(
1857 +                         NS(
1858 +                             surface_id=face,
1859 +                             bottom_left_mm=(0, 0, 0),
1860 +                             bottom_right_mm=(target[0], 0, 0),
1861 +                             top_left_mm=(0, target[1], 0),
1862 +                         )
1863 +                         for face in ("left", "front", "right", "bottom")
1864 +                     )
1865 +                 ),
1866 +             ),
1867 +             geometric_calibration=(front, profile) if face == "bottom" else (profile,),
1868 +             uniform_layouts=layouts,
1869 +         )
1870 +         snapshot = InstanceSnapshot(
1871 +             "image",
1872 +             "image",
1873 +             True,
1874 +             (("width", 999), ("height", 888)),
1875 +             NS(kind="image", fit=fit, asset_id="asset"),
1876 +         )
1877 +         evidence = draw_layer(
1878 +             moderngl,
1879 +             NS(quad_array=quad, layer_program=program),
1880 +             artifact,
1881 +             {"generation:asset": [texture]},
1882 +             "out",
1883 +             "map",
1884 +             snapshot,
1885 +             [(0, 0)] * 4,
1886 +         )
1887 +         assert {item.binding_id for item in evidence} == {
1888 +             "clip_vertices",
1889 +             "image_target_pixels",
1890 +         } | ({"image_fold_map"} if fit == "fold" else set())
1891 +         assert program["image_target_pixels"].value == (
1892 +             target[::-1] if face == "bottom" else target
1893 +         )
1894 +         actual = np.frombuffer(
1895 +             target_fbo.read(components=4, dtype="f4"), dtype="f4"
1896 +         ).reshape(target[1], target[0], 4)[::-1]
1897 +         expected = np.zeros_like(actual)
1898 +         (sx, sy, w, h), (dx, dy, _, _) = source_rectangles(size, target)
1899 +         expected[dy : dy + h, dx : dx + w] = pixels[sy : sy + h, sx : sx + w]
1900 +         if fit == "resize":
1901 +             xs = ((np.arange(target[0]) + 0.5) * size[0] / target[0]).astype(int)
1902 +             ys = ((np.arange(target[1]) + 0.5) * size[1] / target[1]).astype(int)
1903 +             expected = pixels[ys[:, None], xs]
1904 +         if fit == "fold":
1905 +             expected.fill(0)
1906 +             start_x = target[0] + max(0, -(target[0] + (target[0] - target[1]) / 2))
1907 +             for y in range(target[1]):
1908 +                 for x in range(target[0]):
1909 +                     sx, sy = int(start_x + x + 0.5), y
1910 +                     if face == "bottom":
1911 +                         bx = target[0] + (target[0] - target[1]) / 2
1912 +                         sx, sy = (
1913 +                             int(max(0, bx) + y + 0.5),
1914 +                             target[1] + target[0] - 1 - x,
1915 +                         )
1916 +                     if sx < size[0] and sy < size[1]:
1917 +                         expected[y, x] = pixels[sy, sx]
1918 +         np.testing.assert_array_equal(actual, expected)
1919 +     finally:
1920 +         context.release()
1921 +

1923 + def test_shared_context_wait_uses_unsigned_64_bit_ignored_timeout(monkeypatch):
1924 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.capture_sync import order_shared_outputs
1925 +
1926 +     gl = ModuleType("OpenGL.GL")
1927 +     events = []
1928 +     gl.GL_SYNC_GPU_COMMANDS_COMPLETE = 1
1929 +     gl.GL_TIMEOUT_IGNORED = -9223372036854775807
1930 +     gl.glFenceSync = lambda *_args: object()
1931 +     gl.glFlush = lambda: None
1932 +     gl.glWaitSync = lambda fence, flags, timeout: events.append((flags, timeout))
1933 +     gl.glDeleteSync = lambda fence: None
1934 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL.GL", gl)
1935 +     root = ModuleType("OpenGL")
1936 +     root.GL = gl
1937 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", root)
1938 +     pending = []
1939 +     order_shared_outputs(
1940 +         {"one": NS(activate=lambda: None), "two": NS(activate=lambda: None)}, pending
1941 +     )
1942 +     assert events == [(0, 18446744073709551615)] * 2
1943 +     assert not pending
1944 +

1946 + @pytest.mark.parametrize(
1947 +     ("pixel_format", "dtype"),
1948 +     [("rgba8_top_down", "f1"), ("r10g10b10a2_le_top_down", "f4")],
1949 + )
1950 + def test_review_capture_clears_framebuffer_in_creation_order(
1951 +     monkeypatch, pixel_format, dtype
1952 + ):
1953 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.capture import ReviewCapture
1954 +
1955 +     gl = ModuleType("OpenGL.GL")
1956 +     gl.GL_PIXEL_PACK_BUFFER = 1
1957 +     gl.glBindBuffer = lambda *_args: None
1958 +
1959 +     def stop(*_args):
1960 +         raise RuntimeError("reached PBO allocation")
1961 +
1962 +     gl.glGenBuffers = stop
1963 +     root = ModuleType("OpenGL")
1964 +     root.GL = gl
1965 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL", root)
1966 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "OpenGL.GL", gl)
1967 +     modern = ModuleType("moderngl")
1968 +     modern.TRIANGLE_STRIP = 1
1969 +     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "moderngl", modern)
1970 +     events = []
1971 +     texture = NS()
1972 +     fbo = NS(
1973 +         use=lambda: events.append("use"), clear=lambda *_args: events.append("clear")
1974 +     )
1975 +     texture_types = []
1976 +
1977 +     def allocate_texture(*_args, **kwargs):
1978 +         texture_types.append(kwargs["dtype"])
1979 +         return texture
1980 +
1981 +     context = NS(
1982 +         texture=allocate_texture,
1983 +         framebuffer=lambda **_kwargs: fbo,
1984 +         program=lambda **_kwargs: {"source_tex": NS(value=None)},
1985 +         buffer=lambda *_args: NS(),
1986 +         vertex_array=lambda *_args, **_kwargs: NS(),
1987 +     )
1988 +     capture = ReviewCapture()
1989 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="reached PBO allocation"):
1990 +         capture.capture(
1991 +             (NS(output_id="one", texture_handle=NS(ctx=context)),),
1992 +             NS(
1993 +                 composite_width=2,
1994 +                 composite_height=2,
1995 +                 tiles=(),
1996 +                 native_pixel_format=pixel_format,
1997 +             ),
1998 +         )
1999 +     assert events == ["use", "clear", "use"]
2000 +     assert texture_types == [dtype, dtype]
2001 +     capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, pixel_format)
2002 +     assert texture_types == [dtype, dtype]
2003 +     other = (
2004 +         "r10g10b10a2_le_top_down"
2005 +         if pixel_format == "rgba8_top_down"
2006 +         else "rgba8_top_down"
2007 +     )
2008 +     with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="cannot change"):
2009 +         capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, other)
2010 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unsupported"):
2011 +         capture._ensure_compositor(context, 2, 2, "unknown")
2012 +
