4695 + def test_surface_projection_matches_direct_projection_and_is_not_served_stale():
4696 +     from tests.visual_stimulus.support import _linked_texture_fixture
4697 +
4698 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.grating_field import (
4699 +         SurfaceProjection,
4700 +         field_on_surface,
4701 +         projected_point,
4702 +     )
4703 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.config.models.program_model import TextureSettings
4704 +
4705 +     geometry, _ = _linked_texture_fixture()
4706 +     from cephvr.gui.protocol_document import blank_program
4707 +     from cephvr.gui.stimulus_presets import add_stimulus
4708 +
4709 +     program = add_stimulus(blank_program(), 0, "Grating", ("Bottom",))
4710 +     program = add_stimulus(program, 0, "Grating", ("Front",))
4711 +     source, target = program.sequence[0].settings
4712 +     assert isinstance(source, TextureSettings)
4713 +     values = dict(x=3.5, y=-2.0, rotation=37.5, frequency=0.07, phase_x=0.3)
4714 +     projection = SurfaceProjection(geometry)
4715 +     first = field_on_surface(source, values, target, geometry, projection)
4716 +     assert first == field_on_surface(source, values, target, geometry, projection)
4717 +     assert first == field_on_surface(source, values, target, geometry)
4718 +     assert projection.point("front", 1, 2) == projected_point(geometry, "front", 1, 2)
4719 +     # A changed geometry is never answered from another geometry's projection.
4720 +     moved = geometry.model_copy(
4721 +         update={
4722 +             "surfaces": tuple(
4723 +                 s.model_copy(update={"bottom_left_mm": (0.0, 0.0, 5.0)})
4724 +                 if s.surface_id == "front"
4725 +                 else s
4726 +                 for s in geometry.surfaces
4727 +             )
4728 +         }
4729 +     )
4730 +     assert projection.point("front", 1, 2) == projected_point(geometry, "front", 1, 2)
4731 +     assert projected_point(moved, "front", 1, 2) != projection.point("front", 1, 2)
4732 +     assert field_on_surface(
4733 +         source, values, target, moved, projection
4734 +     ) == field_on_surface(source, values, target, moved)
4735 +     with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="calibrated selected surface"):
4736 +         projection.point("missing", 0, 0)

4738 +
4739 + class _SparseTexture:
4740 +     def __init__(self, size, data=None):
4741 +         self.size, self.data, self.released = size, data, False
4742 +
4743 +     def write(self, data, alignment=1):
4744 +         self.data = bytes(data)
4745 +
4746 +     def release(self):
4747 +         self.released = True
4748 +
4749 +
4750 + class _SparseOutput:
4751 +     def __init__(self):
4752 +         self.created = []
4753 +         self.context = self
4754 +
4755 +     def texture(self, size, components, data=None, dtype="f1"):
4756 +         assert components == 4 and dtype == "f1"  # RGBA8 cells, not float32.
4757 +         texture = _SparseTexture(size, data)
4758 +         self.created.append(texture)
4759 +         return texture
4760 +
4761 +     def activate(self):
4762 +         pass
4763 +
4764 +
4765 + class _SparseFeed:
4766 +     """In-process stand-in for the helper: generates credited requests on demand."""
4767 +
4768 +     def __init__(self):
4769 +         self.sent, self.failure, self.hold = [], None, False
4770 +         self.layers = None
4771 +         self.credit = self.produced = 0
4772 +
4773 +     def send(self, data, *, deadline_ns):
4774 +         from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.sparse_noise_wire import (
4775 +             decode_credit,
4776 +             decode_job,
4777 +         )
4778 +
4779 +         self.sent.append(data)
4780 +         if data.startswith(b"JOB "):
4781 +             from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.sparse_noise_stream import requests
4782 +
4783 +             self.layers = decode_job(data)
4784 +             self._requests = requests(self.layers)
4785 +         else:
4786 +             self.credit = decode_credit(data)
4787 +
4788 +     def take(self):
4789 +         from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.sparse_noise_stream import (
4790 +             pattern_frame,
4791 +         )
4792 +
4793 +         frames = []
4794 +         while not self.hold and self.produced < self.credit:
4795 +             request = next(self._requests)
4796 +             frames.append(
4797 +                 (
4798 +                     request.sequence,
4799 +                     *pattern_frame(self.layers[request.layer], request.pattern),
4800 +                 )
4801 +             )
4802 +             self.produced += 1
4803 +         return frames
4804 +
4805 +
4806 + def _sparse_ring(lookahead=3, **program):
4807 +     from tests.visual_stimulus.support import make_prepared_trial
4808 +     from tests.visual_stimulus.test_resources import sparse_layer_program
4809 +
4810 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.rendering.sparse_noise_ring import SparseNoiseRing
4811 +
4812 +     plan = make_prepared_trial(sparse_layer_program(**program))
4813 +     output, feed = _SparseOutput(), _SparseFeed()
4814 +     ring = SparseNoiseRing(
4815 +         plan, feed, {"projector/main": output}, lookahead=lookahead, clock_ns=lambda: 0
4816 +     )
4817 +     snapshots = tuple(
4818 +         InstanceSnapshot(s.instance_id, "sparse_noise", True, (), s)
4819 +         for s in plan.epochs[0].settings
4820 +     )
4821 +     return plan, ring, feed, output, snapshots
4822 +
4823 +
4824 + def test_sparse_ring_prefills_one_lookahead_window_per_concurrent_layer():
4825 +     _, ring, feed, output, _ = _sparse_ring(lookahead=3)
4826 +     ring.start(deadline_ns=1)
4827 +     assert ring.window == 6  # Three patterns ahead for each of two layers.
4828 +     ring.prefill(deadline_ns=1)
4829 +     assert ring.received == 6 and feed.sent[-1] == b"CREDIT 6\n"
4830 +     assert all(t.size == (10, 10) for t in output.created)
4831 +
4832 +
4833 + def test_sparse_ring_selects_due_patterns_holds_misses_and_recycles_textures():
4834 +     import hashlib
4835 +
4836 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.sparse_noise import GENERATOR, pattern_rgba
4837 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.sparse_noise_stream import pattern_texels
4838 +
4839 +     _, ring, feed, output, snapshots = _sparse_ring(lookahead=3)
4840 +     ring.start(deadline_ns=1)
4841 +     ring.prefill(deadline_ns=1)
4842 +     first = ring.select(0, 0, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)
4843 +     selection = first[0].image_selection
4844 +     assert (selection.requested_image_index, selection.image_index) == (0, 0)
4845 +     assert selection.disposition == "current" and not selection.repeated
4846 +     assert first[0].sparse_textures[0].data == pattern_texels(ring.layers[0], 0)
4847 +     layer = ring.layers[0]
4848 +     assert (
4849 +         selection.pattern_sha256
4850 +         == hashlib.sha256(pattern_rgba(layer.recipe, layer.grid, 0)).hexdigest()
4851 +     )
4852 +     assert selection.pattern_generator == GENERATOR
4853 +     # Only the first frame showing a pattern carries its hash.
4854 +     again = ring.select(0, 1, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)[0].image_selection
4855 +     assert again.repeated and again.pattern_sha256 is None
4856 +     # Pattern 1 shown: pattern 0 of each layer retires and credit tops up by two.
4857 +     ring.select(0, 300_000_000, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)
4858 +     assert feed.sent[-1] == b"CREDIT 8\n"
4859 +     created = len(output.created)
4860 +     ring.select(0, 600_000_000, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)
4861 +     assert len(output.created) == created  # Retired textures are reused.
4862 +     # The generator falls behind: the newest due resident pattern is held and
4863 +     # marked, and the skipped older ones retire so credit keeps flowing.
4864 +     feed.hold = True
4865 +     held = ring.select(0, 3_000_000_000, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)[0].image_selection
4866 +     assert (held.requested_image_index, held.image_index) == (10, 3)
4867 +     assert held.disposition == "residency_hold" and not held.repeated
4868 +     assert feed.sent[-1] == b"CREDIT 12\n"
4869 +     again = ring.select(0, 3_100_000_000, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)[0].image_selection
4870 +     assert again.repeated
4871 +     # Recovery: each tick jumps to the newest due pattern until it is current.
4872 +     feed.hold = False
4873 +     for _ in range(4):
4874 +         caught = ring.select(0, 3_300_000_000, snapshots, deadline_ns=1)
4875 +     selection = caught[0].image_selection
4876 +     assert (selection.image_index, selection.disposition) == (11, "current")
4877 +     ring.release()
4878 +     assert all(t.released for t in output.created)
4879 +
4880 +
4881 + def test_sparse_ring_without_a_first_pattern_shows_background_only():

4952 + def test_fold_crop_covers_fractional_bottom_and_rejects_small_source(monkeypatch):
4953 +     from types import SimpleNamespace as NS
4954 +
4955 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources import image_crops
4956 +     from cephvr.visual_stimulus.resources.image_source import ImageSource
4957 +
4958 +     profile = NS(
4959 +         mapping_id="bottom",
4960 +         output_id="out",
4961 +         surface_id="bottom",
4962 +         viewport=NS(width=100, height=80),
4963 +         valid_area=None,
4964 +     )
4965 +     artifact = NS(geometric_calibration=(profile,), display=NS(geometry=None))
4966 +     monkeypatch.setattr(
4967 +         image_crops,
4968 +         "screen_image_frame",
4969 +         lambda p: ([(-1, -1), (1, -1), (1, 1), (-1, 1)], (100, 80)),
4970 +     )
4971 +     monkeypatch.setattr(
4972 +         image_crops,
4973 +         "calibrated_fold_map",
4974 +         lambda *a: (80.25, 90.75, 0, -75.5, -60.25, 0),
4975 +     )
4976 +     source = ImageSource("image", 120, 120, 1, 1, lambda check: b"")
4977 +     crop = image_crops.fold_crop(artifact, "bottom", source)
4978 +     assert (crop.x, crop.y, crop.width, crop.height) == (0, 0, 100, 80)
